──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Simple Aam Papad Challi
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A zesty fusion of tangy aam papad and crunchy nuts, delivering a burst of sweet and sour flavors in every satisfying bite.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 0-5 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet & Sour Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• Few pieces regular aam papad Few pieces kala aam papad
• 1 corn on cob Oil for drizzling Butter for brushing
• 1 teaspoon chaat masala + for sprinkling
• 2 tablespoons lemon juice
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Peel corn on cob and roast directly on high flame.
2. Warm a non-stick grill pan.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
4. Cut roasted corn on cob into 3 portions, halve and place on the hot pan.
5. Drizzle some oil and toss well.
6. Brush the corn pieces with some butter, add red chilli powder, black salt, chaat masala and lemon juice.
7. Top with regular and kala aam papad pieces and cook until soft.
8. Spoon into a serving bowl, sprinkle chaat masala and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Almond Milk Blend
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Enriched with almonds and garnished with sabza and blueberries, this smoothie is a power house of essential nutrients.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Beverages Prep: 6-7 hour Cook: 0-5 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup almonds, soaked overnight and drained
• 2 medium ripe banana
• 1 cup fresh blueberries + for garnish
• 1 teaspoon vanilla essence
• 3 tablespoons honey Fresh mint sprigs for garnish
• 2 tbsps basil seeds (sabza), soaked

Method
1. Put soaked almonds in a grinder jar, add 2 cups warm water and grind till mixture is smooth.
2. Line a strainer kept on a bowl with muslin cloth, pour the ground almond mixture into it and strain the almond milk into the bowl.
3. Grind the residue again with 1 cup warm water to get more almond milk and strain this into the same bowl.
4. Peel the bananas, break into half and put in the grinder jar.
5. Add blueberries, vanilla essence, honey, almond milk and grind to a smooth mixture.
6. Skew blueberries and a mint sprig on each skewer.
7. Put some basil seeds in each serving glass and pour the smoothie over them.
8. Place one skewer on each glass and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Creamy Almond Phirni
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This recipe is specially for those who are intolerant to lactose, so almond milk is used instead of milk

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 1-1.30 hour Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 3 cups Almond milk
• 4 tablespoons Rice, soaked for
• 0 minutes and drained a large pi to garnish Saffron 1/
• 2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder 3/
• 4 cup Jaggery, chopped 1/
• 2 tablespoon Rose water to garnish Blanched pistachios, slivered

Method
1. Warm almond milk in a deep non-stick pan and let it come to a boil.
2. Reduce heat and let it simmer for 8-10 minutes.
3. Put rice in a blender jar, add ¼ cup water and blend to a coarse paste.
4. Add this paste to almond milk and stir until combined.
5. Add a large pinch of saffron and stir until combined and cook until the rice is cooked and the mixture thickens.
6. Add green cardamom powder and stir until combined.
7. Cook on low heat for 3-4 minutes, stirring constantly.
8. Add jaggery and stir until combined.
9. Cook until the jaggery is completely dissolved.
10. Add rose water and stir until combined.
11. Remove the pan from the heat.
12. Pour phirni into individual earthen bowls, sprinkle pistachio slivers on top and garnish with a few strands of saffron and serve chilled.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Aloo Masala Cheese Crisps
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Here, we’re giving a flavourful makeover to leftover aloo sabzi with our easy recipe for 'Aloo Masala Cheese Crisps.' So, follow our step-by-step guide to easily recreate it and enjoy a satisfying...

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Cook: 6-8 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 1½ cups leftover aloo sabzi Grated mixed cheese to sprinkle
• 4 white bread slices
• 2 tablespoons mayonnaise 1½ tablespoons tomato ketchup
• 2 teaspoons chopped fresh parsley + to sprinkle Black sesame seeds to sprinkle
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take leftover aloo sabzi in a bowl.
2. Add mayonnaise, tomato ketchup and parsley and stir until combined.
3. Heat the oven at 180°C.
4. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
5. Arrange the bread slices on baking tray and spread the potato mixture evenly.
6. Sprinkle mixed cheese and bake in preheated oven for 6-8 minutes.
7. Sprinkle black sesame seeds and parsley.
8. Cut into halves and serve.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Fresh Aloo Muri
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Satisfy your snack cravings with 'Fresh Aloo Muri', a beloved street-side delight from Meghalaya. This moreish recipe features crispy puffed rice, spiced mashed potatoes, a special homemade masala...

Cuisine: Meghalaya Course: Snacks and Starters Cook: 6-8 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy

Ingredients
• 3 medium potatoes (aloo), boiled and peeled
• 4 cups puffed rice (muri)
• 2 tablespoons coriander seeds
• 1 tablespoon cumin seeds
• 1 medium onion, chopped
• 2 green chillies, chopped
• 3 tablespoons mustard oil 2-
• 3 tablespoons chopped fresh coriander leaves + to sprinkle ¼ cup aloo bhujiya + to sprinkle ½ lemon
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm a nonstick pan.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add coriander seeds, cumin seeds and fry gently for 2-3 minutes.
4. Place in a mortar and crush to fine powder with a mortar.
5. Put a bowl, cut the potatoes into medium sized pieces and add to the bowl.
6. Add onion, green chillies, puffed rice, 2-3 tbsps ground spice mix, tamarind water, mustard oil and stir until combined.
7. Add coriander and aloo bhujiya and stir until combined.
8. Squeeze lemon and mix.
9. Place in serving bowls, sprinkle coriander, aloo bhujiya and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Anjeer and Boondi Hotcakes
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A delectable fusion of traditional Indian flavors, featuring sweet figs and crunchy boondi, wrapped in a fluffy pancake for a satisfying breakfast treat.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 41-50 minutes Cook: 51-60 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 dried figs, soaked and chopped ¼ cup sweet boondi
• 2 cups refined flour (maida) ½ teaspoon baking soda ¼ teaspoon vanilla essence 1¾ cups milk
• 2 tablespoons chopped jaggery 15-
• 0 walnuts Butter for shallow frying
• 8 whole dried figs, soaked Rabri for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take refined flour in a bowl, add baking soda and vanilla essence.
2. Warm 1½ cups milk in a deep non-stick pan stirring continuously till the milk comes to a boil.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
4. Add jaggery, switch off heat and stir until combined till jaggery melts.
5. Pour the milk mixture into the refined flour mixture.
6. Add chopped figs, boondi and remaining milk.
7. Add little water and stir until combined to make a medium thick and smooth batter.
8. Chop and add walnuts and stir until combined.
9. Set aside to rest for 10 minutes.
10. Warm butter in a non-stick tawa, pour a ladle of batter and spread into a medium sized pancake.
11. Place a whole fig in centre of and cook on medium heat for 2-3 minutes, turnover and cook on other side till golden brown on both sides.
12. Move onto a serving plate and garnish with rabri.
13. Serve at once.

Anjeer aur Khajur Shake
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Healthy and flavourful Milkshake made using Anjeer aur Khajur is absolutely easy to make. Especially during hot summer, a glass full of this milkshake gives an instant boost of energy.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Beverages Cook: 0 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet

Ingredients
• 10-
• 5 dried figs 10-
• 5 dates
• 2 cups milk 2-
• 3 tablespoons honey Fusion Omega Boost Trail Mix to sprinkle

Method
1. Take Zahidi Whole Natural Dates in a bowl and deseed.
2. Add sufficient water and soak for 6-8 hours.
3. Take Select Figs in a bowl and deseed.
4. Add sufficient water and soak for 6-8 hours.
5. Drain the water from dates and figs and transfer in a blender jar.
6. Add milk, honey and blend to fine mixture.
7. Place in individual glasses and sprinkle Fusion Omega Boost Trail Mix on top.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Comfort Anjeer Toffee
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A healthy sweet made without the addition of sugar – ideal for growing children

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 3.30-4 hour Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 5 Dried figs (anjeer), soaked for 2-
• 3 hours and drained
• 1 tbsp + for greasing Ghee
• 1 cup Seedless dates (khajur), chopped 4-
• 5 Walnuts
• 2 tablespoons Cashewnuts, chopped
• 2 tablespoons Pistachios, chopped
• 2 tablespoons Almonds, chopped 1/
• 2 cup Gum resin (gond), fried

Method
1. Put the figs in a grinder jar, add a little water and grind to fine paste.
2. Warm ghee in a deep non-stick pan and add fig paste and fry gently on medium heat for 3-4 minutes.
3. Add dates, stir until combined and cook for 10-15 minutes or till excess moisture dries up.
4. Chop walnuts and add to the pan along with cashewnuts, pistachios and almonds.
5. Stir until combined and cook until mixture begins to leave the sides of the pan.
6. Add gond and stir until combined.
7. Cook for 3-4 minutes on medium heat.
8. Set aside in a bowl and allow to cool.
9. Refrigerate for 30 minutes.
10. Grease your palms with ghee and shape the mixture into oblong or any other desired shape.
11. Take strips of parchment paper and place a toffee on each paper and roll them.
12. Take coloured toffee sheets and roll the toffees again.
13. Store the toffees in a glass jar or serve.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Arugula and Apple Toss
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A peppery, distinctive-tasting green combined with apple slices and dressed with some sauces for a vibrant blend of flavors and textures — arugula and Apple Salad.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Salads Cook: 0 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 0 grams arugula leaves
• 1 red apple
• 1 green apple
• 1 English cucumber 1½ teaspoons mustard paste
• 1 lemon
• 1 ½ tablespoons honey
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Cut red and green apple into quarters.
2. Deseed and slice.
3. Halve and slice English cucumber.
4. Take ice cold water in a bowl.
5. Add arugula leaves and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
6. Place in a salad rinser and drain excess water.
7. Take mustard paste in a glass jar.
8. Squeeze lemon, add honey, extra virgin olive oil, crushed black peppercorns and salt.
9. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
10. Close the lid and shake well.
11. Take cucumber in a bowl.
12. Add green apple and red apple.
13. Reserve some dressing and drizzle prepared dressing and stir until combined.
14. Arrange some arugula leaves on a serving plate.
15. Add some apple mixture on top.
16. Sprinkle pecans and dried cranberries on top.
17. Arrange remaining arugula leaves and apple mixture on top.
18. Sprinkle some more pecans and dried cranberries on top.
19. Drizzle remaining dressing and serve.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Avocado Crisps
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Toasted sourdough topped with avocado, cream cheese, and mixed seeds.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Cook: 0-5 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 2 avocados ½ sourdough bread loaf
• 4 tablespoons mixed seeds Micro greens for garnish Extra virgin olive oil for drizzling
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Cut the sourdough bread loaf into thick slices.
2. Warm a cast iron pan.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
4. Add butter and let it melt.
5. Place the sourdough slices on the pan and toast on both sides on low heat till golden and crisp.
6. Take slices off the heat and arrange on a worktop.
7. Cut the avocados in half, remove the seed, peel, and cut each half of the avocados into thin slices.
8. Apply some cream cheese over one side of the toasted bread slices.
9. Arrange the avocado slices over them and sprinkle crushed black peppercorns and salt.
10. Garnish with pickled onion rings.
11. Sprinkle mixed seeds, garnish with micro greens, and drizzle extra virgin olive oil.
12. Serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Spiced Babycorn Paneer Drumsticks
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A savory symphony of textures and flavors, marrying the tender crunch of babycorn wrapped with creamy paneer and potato mixture, all rolled into one flavourful bite.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 baby corns
• 1 cup grated cottage cheese (paneer)
• 2 medium potatoes, boiled, peeled and grated
• 1 small carrot, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon chopped ginger
• 1 teaspoon chopped garlic 2-
• 3 green chillies, chopped
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder
• 1 teaspoon garam masala powder
• 1 tablespoon refined flour (maida)
• 1 tablespoon +
• 2 red cherry tomatoes for garnish Spring onion flower for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take paneer in a bowl, add potatoes, carrot, ginger, garlic, green chillies, onion, red chilli powder, garam masala powder, red chilli sauce and soy sauce and stir until combined.
2. Add refined flour, 1 tablespoon cornflour and salt and stir until combined.
3. Cover ¾ of each baby corn with the mixture to form drumsticks.
4. Place in plate and add ½ cup cornflour and coat well.
5. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai, slide in the baby corn drumsticks and deep fry on low heat till golden and crisp.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
7. Drain on absorbent paper.
8. Sprinkle chaat masala.
9. Arrange them on a serving platter, sprinkle some more chaat masala, garnish with red cherry tomatoes and spring onion flower.
10. Serve warm with red chilli sauce.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Simple Bajra Risotto
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Healthy version of risotto – bajra is a good replacement for Arborio rice since it has a similar bite

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Main Prep: 10-15 hours Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 1/
• 2 cups pearl millet (bajra),, soaked for
• 2 hours, drained and cooked with v
• 1 tablespoon olive oil
• 2 tablespoons butter 1/
• 2 medium onion, peeled
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic
• 1 cup white wine 3-
• 0 grams parmesan cheese
• 2 1/
• 2 tablespoons fresh cream Red chilli flakes to sprinkle Fresh parsley leaves to garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm olive oil in a non-stick pan, add butter and let it melt.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Finely chop onion and add to the pan, fry gently till translucent.
4. Add garlic and fry gently well for 2 minutes.
5. Add white wine, stir until combined and cook for 2-3 minutes.
6. Add cooked bajra and stir until combined.
7. Cook for 2-3 minutes.
8. Add thyme sprigs and crushed black peppercorns and stir until combined.
9. Grate parmesan cheese into the risotto and add fresh cream and stir until combined.
10. Cook for 1-2 minutes.
11. Spoon into a serving bowl, sprinkle chilli flakes and crushed black peppercorns.
12. Garnish with parsley and serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Classic Baked Chocolate Apple
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Apples hollowed out, stuffed with fresh coconut and jaggery mixture, baked and served sprinkled with chocolate chips

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 4 medium Red apples to garnish Chocolate chips to sprinkle Sea salt

Method
1. Preheat oven to 160º C.
2. Slice the top and bottom of the apples and scoop out the flesh.
3. Sprinkle sea salt and rub it uniformly inside the hollowed apples and place them on a baking tray.
4. Chop the flesh.
5. Warm a non-stick pan, add the chopped apple flesh and fry gently till lightly cooked.
6. Add coconut, mixed nuts and jaggery and stir until combined.
7. Cook on low heat till jaggery melts.
8. Switch off heat.
9. Add green cardamom powder and cinnamon powder and stir until combined.
10. Wipe the apples from inside with a tissue if they are moist from inside.
11. Fill the nut mixture into each cored apple.
12. Break half the caramel bars into small pieces and place on top, drizzle some rabdi.
13. Repeat the process once more.
14. Place the tray in the preheated oven and bake for 12-15 minutes.
15. Spread remaining stuffing in deep serving plate and place the baked apples over it.
16. Garnish with rabdi, chocolate chips and mixed nuts and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Banana Loaf
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Savor the warmth of home baking in every slice of our Banana Loaf – a comforting embrace of ripe bananas and nostalgia.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 8-10 hour Cook: 51-60 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 medium ripe bananas
• 0 grams almonds, soaked overnight and peeled ¼ cup butter, cut into cubes ½ teaspoon cinnamon powder ½ teaspoon vanilla essence ½ tablespoon lemon juice
• 1 tablespoon maple syrup A pinch of baking soda A pinch of sea salt
• 2 eggs
• 1 tablespoon flax seeds (alsi)
• 1 tablespoon chocolate chips
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Preheat oven to 110º C.
2. Spread the almonds on one baking tray and bananas on another baking tray and place both the trays in the preheated oven and bake for 30 minutes.
3. Remove them from the oven, increase the temperature to 200º C.
4. Peel the bananas, put into a bowl and mash with a fork.
5. Add butter and stir until combined, add cinnamon powder, vanilla essence, lemon juice, maple syrup, baking soda and salt.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
7. Add eggs and stir until combined until well combined.
8. Put toasted almonds in a mixer jar, add flax seeds and grind into a fine powder.
9. Add this powder to the banana and egg mixture and fold in gently.
10. Pour the mixture into a silicon loaf mould, level the surface and sprinkle chocolate chips on top.
11. Place the mould in the preheated oven and bake for 20-25 minutes.
12. Check the bread by inserting a toothpick in the centre and if it comes out clean the bread is done.
13. Remove the mould from the oven and let it cool.
14. Slice the bread.
15. Wrap the slices in Freshwrapp and pack it in the tiffin box.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Banana Walnut Chocolate Frozen Cream
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

The combination of ripe bananas, walnuts and chocolate make for a super flavourful ice cream that will win many a heart

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 10-15 hour Cook: 0-5 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 3 medium Ripe bananas, peeled and frozen 3/
• 4 cup Walnuts
• 4 tablespoons Unsweetened cocoa powder 1/
• 2 cup Fresh cream
• 3 tablespoons Honey
• 1 teaspoon Vanilla essence to garnish Ripe banana, peeled and coated with melted dark chocolate clove Sugar fondant to garnish Walnuts, crushed

Method
1. Cut each banana into 3 pieces and put in a grinder jar.
2. Add cocoa powder, cream, honey, walnuts and vanilla essence and grind into a smooth mixture.
3. Pour this mixture into an air tight container and put the lid on.
4. Keep it in the freezer for 6-8 hours or overnight.
5. Once set take the ice cream out of the freezer.
6. Dip ice cream scooper in warm water and then scoop out 5 scoops of ice cream into a serving bowl.
7. Skew the banana coated with melted dark chocolate onto a skewer and place it on the rim of the bowl.
8. Use sugar fondant to create eyes and nose of a penguin.
9. Garnish with crushed walnuts and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Comfort Barley Risotto
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Barley replaces Arborio rice in this risotto and the result is very tasty. Barley cooked with garlic, assorted mushrooms and served.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Main Prep: 8-10 hours Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 1/
• 2 cups barley (jau), soaked for
• 8 hours and cooked
• 1 tablespoon olive oil
• 1 tablespoon butter 6-
• 8 garlic cloves, chopped
• 1 medium onion, finely choppoed 4-
• 5 button mushrooms
• 1 cup white wine 7-
• 2 cup fresh cream
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm olive oil and butter in a deep non-stick till butter melts.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add garlic and fry gently well.
4. Add onion, stir until combined and saute till translucent.
5. Quarter button mushrooms and add to the onion mixture.
6. Add white wine, stir until combined and cook for 1-2 minutes.
7. Add shitake mushrooms and stir until combined.
8. Cover and cook for 4-6 minutes.
9. Add cooked barley and stir until combined.
10. Add salt.
11. Grate processed cheese directly into the barley mixture and stir until combined.
12. Add crushed black peppercorns, stir until combined and cook for 1-2 minutes.
13. Add fresh cream and stir until combined.
14. Cook for 1 minute and take the pan off the heat.
15. Spoon into a serving bowl, sprinkle crushed black peppercorns and serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Kitchen Batata Puri
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Call them batata puri or sabudana vada, they are just as flavourful

Cuisine: Indian Course: Breads Prep: 8-10 hour Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 4 Medium potatoes medium, boiled and peeled
• 1 cup Sago (sabudana) soaked 1/
• 2 cup Roasted peanuts crushed
• 2 tablespoons Fresh coriander leaves chopped
• 1 tablespoon Ginger-green chilli paste 1/
• 2 tablespoon Lemon juice
• 2 Banana leaves cut into medium squares to deep-fry Oil to serve Coconut chutney
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take sago in a bowl add sago, add peanuts, coriander leaves, sugar, rock salt, ginger green chilli paste and lemon juice.
2. Mash and add potatoes and stir until combined.
3. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
4. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
5. Divide the mixture into equal portions.
6. Place a banana leaf square on the worktop, apply little oil on it, place a portion of potato mixture, gently press and flatten slightly.
7. Slide into hot oil and deep-fry, turning sides, till golden brown on both sides.
8. Drain on absorbent paper.
9. Arrange the puris on a serving plate and serve warm with coconut chutney.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Everyday Bathue ka Parantha
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Bathua leaves kneaded with whole wheat flour, rolled into paranthas and cooked

Cuisine: Indian Course: Breads Prep: 26-30 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 medium bunches Bathua leaves, cleaned
• 1 1/
• 2 cups for dusting Whole wheat flour
• 1 teaspoon Carom seeds (ajwain) 2-
• 3 Green chillies, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon Ginger, finely chopped 1/
• 2 tsp + to sprinkle Red chilli powder 1/
• 2 teaspoon Turmeric powder
• 1 medium Onion, chopped as required Ghee to sprinkle Chaat masala to serve White butter to serve Yogurt to serve Pickle
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Roughly chop bathua leaves and transfer into a bowl.
2. Add carom seeds, green chillies, ginger, red chilli powder, turmeric powder, wheat flour and onion and stir until combined.
3. Add water as required and knead to a soft dough.
4. Divide the dough into equal portions and roll out into large rotis on a dusted worktop.
5. Apply some ghee on the rotis, dust some flour and sprinkle chilli powder and chaat masala on top.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
7. Fold into a square and roll out into paranthas.
8. Warm a non-stick tawa.
9. Place the parathas on it, one at a time, and cook, basting with ghee, till evenly golden brown on both the sides.
10. Place them on individual serving plates and serve warm with white butter, yogurt and pickle of your choice.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Creamy Beetroot Browines
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

These extremely healthy and dark pink brownies are simply moreish

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup Boiled beetroot puree for greasing Butter
• 3 tablespoons Flax seed (alsi) powder
• 1 Ripe banana, roasted 3/
• 4 cup Honey 3/
• 4 cup Coconut oil
• 1 cup Rice flour 3/
• 4 cup Cocoa powder
• 1 pinch Salt
• 1 teaspoon Baking powder 1/
• 2 cup Walnuts, chopped

Method
1. Preheat oven to 200º C.
2. Grease a square baking tin with butter and line it with butter paper.
3. Take flax seed powder in a large bowl, add 9 tablespoons water and stir until combined, set aside for 5 minutes.
4. Peel the roasted banana and mash it with a fork.
5. Add the banana to the flax seed mixture.
6. Add beetroot puree and stir until combined.
7. Add honey and coconut oil and whisk till well combined.
8. Sift rice flour into the beetroot mixture and fold in gently.
9. Sift cocoa powder into the beetroot mixture and fold in again.
10. Add salt, baking powder and walnuts and stir until combined.
11. Pour the batter into the prepared cake tin, keep it in the preheated oven and bake for 20-25 minutes.
12. Remove the tin from the oven and let it cool.
13. Cut it into squares and pack it in the tiffin.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Kitchen Beetroot Coconut Barfi
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

These beautiful ruby red barfis are so tasty that they will get consumed in a jiffy

Cuisine: Maharashtrian Course: Mithais Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 medium Beetroot, peeled and grated
• 2 cups Fresh coconut, scraped
• 1 tbsp _ for greasing Ghee
• 1 cup Sugar 1/
• 2 cup Fresh cream to garnish Walnuts
• 3 tablespoons Powdered sugar 1/
• 2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder

Method
1. Grease a barfi tray with some ghee.
2. Warm ghee in a deep non-stick pan, add sugar, coconut and cream and stir until combined.
3. Cook on medium heat, stirring continuously, for 15-20 minutes.
4. Meanwhile roughly chop walnuts.
5. When mixture begins to leave the pan, add the beetroot and stir until combined.
6. Add powdered sugar and green cardamom powder, stir until combined and cook on medium heat for 2-3 minutes.
7. Transfer the barfi mixture in the greased tray and spread it evenly.
8. Sprinkle walnuts on top.
9. Set aside till set and cooled down to room temperature.
10. Cut into rectangular barfis.
11. Insert a coloured ice cream stick in each barfi, arrange them on a serving platter and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Wholesome Beetroot Quinoa Pachadi
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Popular South Indian accompaniment made with all super foods. Watch this video to find out how to make this recipe. This is

Cuisine: South Indian Course: Accompaniments Cook: 10-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 4 tablespoons beetroot puree ¼ cup quinoa
• 1 teaspoons oil ¼ cup scraped fresh coconut
• 1 green chillies, finely chopped ½ inch ginger, finely chopped
• 2 dried red chillies ½ teaspoon mustard seeds 4-
• 5 curry leaves
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm a small deep non-stick pan.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add quinoa, 1 cup water and salt and mix.
4. Add 1 teaspoon oil, mix and bring the mixture to boil.
5. Cover and cook until quinoa is done.
6. Place in a bowl and refrigerate it.
7. Coarsely grind together coconut, green chillies and ginger.
8. Take yogurt in another bowl and whisk.
9. Add ground coconut mixture and whisk.
10. Add beetroot puree and whisk well.
11. Add salt to yogurt mixture and whisk.
12. Add cooked quinoa and whisk well.
13. Transfer on a shallow serving plate.
14. To prepare tempering, heat remaining oil in a tempering pan.
15. Roughly cut dried red chillies using a pair of scissors.
16. Add mustard seeds to hot oil and let it sputter.
17. Add curry leaves and let it crackle.
18. Add dried red chillies and swirl the pan for few seconds.
19. Pour the tempering over the beetroot yogurt mixture and serve.

Bhakri Flatbake
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A creative fusion dish that combines the rustic charm of traditional Indian bhakri with the flavorful toppings of a classic pizza, offering a unique culinary experience.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• Readymade pearl millet (bajra) dough Pearl millet (bajra) flour for dusting
• 1 teaspoon oil ½ teaspoon cumin seeds
• 1 teaspoon chopped garlic
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 medium red capsicum, cut into triangles
• 1 medium green capsicum, cut into triangles ½ cup boiled corn kernels Cheese sauce as required Halved black olives for garnish Black olive slices for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. To prepare pizza sauce, heat oil in a non-stick pan, add cumin seeds and garlic and stir until combined.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add onion, mix and fry gently till translucent.
4. Add tomato puree, turmeric powder, red chilli powder and salt and stir until combined.
5. Fry gently till oil separates.
6. Sprinkle bajra flour on worktop, keep the dough on it and roll into a thick disc.
7. Warm a non-stick tawa and place the disc on it and cook for 3-4 minutes.
8. Flip and cook on other side till golden brown specs appear.
9. While the bhakri is still on the pan, spread a spoonful of pizza sauce on it, sprinkle mozzarella cheese in the centre, sprinkle red and green capsicum triangles, boiled corn kernels on top.
10. Cover and cook on low heat till cheese melts completely.
11. Transfer the pizza on a serving plate.
12. Put cheese sauce in a piping bag and pipe out cheese sauce on top of pizza in the shape of spider web.
13. Place a halved black olive in centre.
14. Place 6 halved olives around.
15. Now place black olive slices as legs of the spider.
16. Serve warm.

Fresh Bhakri with Thecha
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Typical rural meal of Maharashtra – it is so very tasty that you will want to eat it again and again

Cuisine: Maharashtrian Course: Breads Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 31-40 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 3 tablespoons Oil 10-
• 2 cloves Garlic 10-
• 2 Dark green chillies 10-
• 4 cup Roasted peanuts
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. To make bhakri, heat sufficient water in an earthen pot, and let it come to a boil.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Take ragi flour in a large bowl, add salt and stir until combined.
4. Pour the hot water little at a time, stir until combined till it forms soft dough.
5. Divide the dough into equal portions and shape each into a ball.
6. Dust each ball with ragi flour, flatten it and roll into a thin disc.
7. Warm an earthen tawa, place the discs on it, one at a time, and cook, turning sides, till small brown specks appear on both sides.
8. Take it off the heat and keep under a muslin cloth.
9. To make thecha, heat oil in another earthen pot, add garlic, dark green chillies, light green chillies and sea salt and fry gently well.
10. Cover and cook for 12-15 minutes, stirring occasionally.
11. Remove the lid and add roasted peanuts, adjust salt and fry gently well.
12. Cook for 2-3 minutes and take it off the heat.
13. Cool the mixture, transfer into a mortar pestle and pound it coarsely.
14. Arrange the bhakris on a serving platter and transfer thecha into a bowl and serve the two together.

Wholesome Bhindi Hara Masala
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Bursting with flavour this bhindi dish is so very moreish. Ladyfingers cooked with a green masala and Indian spices.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Main Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 grams ladyfingers (bhindi), ends trimmed and cut into
• 1 inch pieces
• 1 tbsp oil + to deep fry
• 1 inch ginger, cut into strips + to garnish
• 1 teaspoon cumin seeds 2-
• 3 green chillies, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic
• 2 small onions, finely chopped
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder 1/
• 4 teaspoon turmeric powder 1/
• 2 medium tomatoes
• 1 green chilli, slit Yellow capsicum strips to garnish Red capsicum strips to garnish Hara masala
• 1 cup fresh coriander leaves 1/
• 2 cup fresh mint leaves 1/
• 4 cup scraped fresh coconut
• 2 green chillies
• 1 inch ginger
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai and deep-fry the ladyfinger pieces till crisp.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Drain on absorbent paper.
4. To make hara masala, put coriander leaves, mint leaves, coconut, green chillies and ginger in a grinder jar and grind to a coarse paste.
5. Warm 1 tbsp oil in a non-stick pan, add ginger, cumin seeds and green chillies and fry gently till fragrant.
6. Add garlic, mix and fry gently till it turns brown.
7. Add onions, mix and fry gently till translucent.
8. Add the fried ladyfingers, red chilli powder, turmeric powder, dried mango powder and salt, mix and cook for 2-3 minutes.
9. Add hara masala and stir until combined.
10. Roughly chop tomatoes, add to the pan and cook until they turn soft and pulpy.
11. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with ginger strips, slit green chilli, yellow and red capsicum strips and serve warm.

Comfort Bhindi Methi Bhurjee
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Bhindi and methi make odd couple but in this dish they are simply very compatible. Ladyfingers and fenugreek leaves cooked with Indian spices. Best served with hot phulkas.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Main Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 0 grams ladyfinges (bhindi), finely chopped
• 1 large bunch fresh fenugreek leaves (methi), chopped
• 3 tablespoons oil 2-
• 3 green chillies, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon ginger, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon garlic, finely chopped
• 2 medium onions, finely chopped
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder 1/
• 2 teaspoon turmeric powder
• 1 tablespoon coriander powder
• 1 teaspoon garam masala powder
• 1 1/
• 1 medium tomato, finely chopped Fresh coriander sprigs to garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm rice bran oil in a non-stick pan, add green chillies, ginger and garlic and fry gently till fragrant.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add onions, mix and fry gently till translucent.
4. Add ladyfingers, mix and cook for 8-10 minutes.
5. Add red chilli powder, turmeric powder, coriander powder, garam masala powder, dried mango powder and salt.
6. Stir until combined and cook for 4-5 minutes.
7. Add fenugreek leaves and tomato, mix and cook until the tomatoes turn soft and pulpy.
8. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with coriander sprigs and serve warm.

Biscuit Frozen Cream Chocolate Bars
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Icecream bar covered with glucose biscuits, frozen and served drizzled with melted chocolate and nuts Icecream bar covered with glucose biscuits, frozen and served drizzled with melted chocolate and...

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 2-2.30 hour Cook: 0-5 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 0 Glucose biscuits
• 1 litres Vanilla ice cream bar
• 1 cup Dark chocolate, melted to garnish Pistachios, finely chopped to garnish Vermicelli sprinklers to garnish Almonds, chopped

Method
1. Stick half the glucose biscuits on the edge of one aide of the ice cream bar.
2. Trim the exposed portion of the bar and turn it sideways so that biscuit portion is on one side.
3. Now halve the bar from middle lengthwise and stick the remaining biscuits on the cut side.
4. Transfer the bar on a parchment sheet and cover it completely.
5. Freeze for 1-2 hours.
6. Further cut into smaller bars.
7. Insert an ice cream stick in centre of each bar.
8. Take melted dark chocolate in a bowl.
9. Pour melted dark chocolate on each bar with a spoon.
10. Drip off extra chocolate.
11. Sprinkle pistachio, vermicelli sprinkles and almonds on each bar.
12. Place them on a silicon mat and refrigerate for 5 minutes.
13. Remove from refrigerator and transfer onto a serving platter and serve at once.

Quick Black Grapes Shikanji

A refreshing and tangy black grape shikanji with a burst of spices, perfect for a chilled, revitalizing drink! This is

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Beverages Cook: 6-8 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy

• 25-
• 0 black grapes + for garnish ¼ cup sugar ¼ teaspoon salt A pinch of red chilli powder ¼ teaspoon roasted cumin powder A pinch of chaat masala Lemon wedges as required Pink salt as required Ice cubes as required Lemon slices as required Grape slices as required Mint sprigs as required Drinking soda as required

Method
1. Transfer black grapes in a blender jar.

Broccoli & Cheese Broth
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This velvety concoction combines the wholesome goodness of tender broccoli florets with the rich, creamy allure of melted cheese, creating a symphony of flavors. This soup gets done easily and...

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Soups Prep: 5-10 minutes Cook: 10-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 medium broccoli 8-
• 2 small pieces of bocconcini
• 3 tablespoons butter
• 1 medium onion 8-
• 0 garlic cloves
• 1 cup milk
• 2 teaspoons olive oil Slices of bread to serve Parsley sprig for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Separate small florets from broccoli and discard their stems.
2. Put butter in a soup maker.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.

Broccoli Mac and Cheese Special
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Blanched broccoli florets tossed in a healthy cheesy white sauce . Macaroni is replaced with broccoli in this recipe.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Main Prep: 8-10 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 4 tablespoons butter
• 1 tablespoon whole wheat flour
• 2 cups milk
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Boil sufficient water in a deep non-stick deep pan.
2. Cut small florets of broccoli.
3. Add salt and broccoli florets to the boiling water and blanch for 1-2 minutes.
4. Meanwhile, melt butter in another non-stick pan, add whole wheat flour and stir until combined.
5. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
6. Add milk and stir until combined.
7. Grate processed cheese directly into the pan, stir until combined and cook until the cheese melts.
8. Add salt and crushed black peppercorns and stir until combined.
9. A pinch of dried mixed herbs and stir until combined.
10. Strain the broccoli and add to the sauce, stir until combined and take the pan off the heat.
11. Spoon into a serving plate, sprinkle red chilli flakes and serve warm.

Burnt Garlic Vegetable Fried Rice Edition
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Rice tossed with fresh cut vegetables and burnt garlic

Cuisine: Indo-Chinese Course: Rice Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 1/
• 2 cups Basmati rice,soaked for 15-
• 0 minutes 10-
• 2 cloves Garlic 5-
• 6 French beans 1/
• 4 medium Cabbage 1/
• 2 medium Carrot 2-
• 3 Spring onion bulbs
• 1 tablespoon Oil
• 1 teaspoon Soy sauce
• 1 teaspoon Vinegar
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Finely chop garlic cloves, string the French beans and chop them finely.
2. Finely shred cabbage, peel and grate carrot.
3. Slice spring onion bulbs and finely chop the spring onion green stalk.
4. Boil sufficient water in a deep pan and add salt.
5. Drain and add the rice cover and cook until the rice is ¾ done.
6. Drain and spread the rice on a plate to cool down.
7. Warm oil in a non-stick wok, add garlic and fry gently on high heat till golden brown.
8. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
9. Add spring onion bulbs, mix and fry gently for 30 seconds.
10. Add French beans, carrot and cabbage, mix and fry gently for 1 minute.
11. Add salt and soy sauce and stir until combined.
12. Add rice and adjust salt, toss and cook for 1 minute.
13. Cover and cook on low heat for 2 minutes.
14. Switch off heat, add vinegar and stir until combined.
15. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with spring onion greens and serve warm.

Creamy Caramel Walnut Fudge
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This fudge will be highly appreciated by both children and adults alike

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 51-60 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 4 tbsps + for drizzling Caramel syrup 1/
• 4 cup Walnuts, chopped
• 1 tbsp + to grease Ghee
• 2 tablespoons Butter 1/
• 2 cup Khoya, grated
• 1 cup Condensed milk
• 0 grams Dark chocolate, chopped

Method
1. Grease a shallow bowl with some ghee.
2. Warm ghee in a non-stick pan, add butter and walnuts, mix and fry gently till the walnuts are roasted.
3. Reduce heat, add khoya and condensed milk, mix and cook until the mixture thickens, stirring continuously.
4. Remove from heat, add caramel syrup and stir until combined.
5. Place the pan back on heat and mix till well combined.
6. Add chocolate and mix till it melts.
7. Transfer the mixture onto a greased bowl and spread evenly.
8. Drizzle some caramel syrup on top and refrigerate till set.
9. Cut into pieces, arrange them on a serving platter and serve.

Fresh Cashew Pesto Pasta
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Get ready to fall in love with 'Fresh Cashew Pesto Pasta' all over again, which features the premium taste of #Prov, making it a flavorful recipe perfect for every occasion!

Cuisine: Italian Course: Noodles and Pastas Cook: 8-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 10-
• 5 cashews 1½ cups fresh basil leaves 8-
• 0 black peppercorns 10-
• 5 garlic cloves 1½ tablespoons parmesan cheese powder ½ cup +
• 2 tablespoons extra virgin olive oil
• 1 tablespoon chopped garlic 4-
• 5 button mushrooms, quartered ¼ cup boiled corn kernels 5-
• 2 cups boiled penne pasta Grated parmesan cheese to sprinkle Fresh basil sprig for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Spiced Chaat Quiche
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A fusion delight combining the flaky goodness of quiche with the tangy, spicy flavors of Indian chaat, perfect for a unique and satisfying snack or appetizer.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 8-10 hour Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• Tart 2½ cups soya bean flour Salt to taste
• 4 tablespoons ghee
• 1 teaspoon carom seeds (ajwain)
• 1 cup black eyed beans (lobia) Filling
• 2 tablespoons powdered sugar ¼ cup roasted peanuts, peeled
• 1 cup dried white peas (matara), soaked overnight, boiled with salt and turmeric powder
• 2 medium sweet potatoes, boiled, peeled and cut into ½ inch cubes
• 1 medium carrot, peeled and grated
• 1 medium beetroot, peeled and grated Chaat masala to sprinkle Tamarind chutney to drizzle Green chutney to drizzle Nylon sev for garnish Blanched carrot, cut into star shaped pieces for garnish Blanched beetroot, cut into star shaped pieces for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Heat the oven to 180˚C.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. To prepare short crust dough, take soyabean flour in a bowl.
4. Add salt, ghee and stir until combined.
5. Add carom seeds.
6. Add little water and knead to semi soft dough.
7. Divide the dough into equal portions, place each on the worktop and roll into a thick disc.
8. Place it in a quiche mould and press lightly on all sides.
9. Trim the edges which are bulging out of the mould.
10. Spread a parchment paper on it and spread black eyed beans over the paper.
11. Place the mould in the preheated oven and blind bake for 12-15 minutes.
12. Take yogurt, salt, black salt, chaat masala and powdered sugar in a bowl.
13. Stir until combined and set aside.
14. Lightly crush roasted peanuts and set aside in another bowl.
15. Remove quiche mould from the oven and allow it to cool down completely.
16. Unmould the quiche base from the mould.
17. Spread boiled matara evenly over it.
18. Add sweet potato cubes, grated carrot and beetroot.
19. Sprinkle chaat masala and crushed peanuts.
20. Pour the yogurt mixture on top and cover the filling completely.
21. Drizzle tamarind chutney and green chutney.
22. Sprinkle nylon sev, garnish with carrot and beetroot stars and serve.

Wholesome Chakna Platter
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

An assortment of spicy and crunchy snacks commonly enjoyed with drinks in India, featuring items like peanuts, fried lentils, chickpea flour noodles (sev), and seasoned nuts or seeds.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 31-40 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• Masala Peanuts
• 1 teaspoon chaat masala Oil for deep-frying Wasabi Makhana
• 1 tablespoon oil
• 2 cups puffed lotus seeds (makhana)
• 2 teaspoons wasabi powder Masala Papad Cones
• 1 papad
• 2 cups chana, boiled
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 tomato, finely chopped
• 1 green chilli, finely chopped ½ teaspoon black salt
• 1 tablespoon chopped coriander Juice of half lemon Sev for garnish Coriander leaves for garnish Cheesy Nuts
• 1 cup cashews, whole
• 1 teaspoon chaat masala Cheeseling Bhel
• 2 cups cheeseling
• 1 medium tomato, finely chopped
• 1 ½ teaspoon chaat masala
• 1 tablespoon chopped coriander ¼ cup sev Schezwan Chakli
• 1 cup rice flour ¼ cup split skinless black gram (dhuli urad dal), ground
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Masala Peanuts Warm sufficient oil in a kadai and deep-fry the peanuts till golden brown.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Transfer to a bowl.
4. In a separate bowl add chilli powder, black salt, salt to taste, chaat masala and prepare a masala for the peanuts.
5. Mix the prepared masala in the peanuts well, transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
6. Wasabi Makhana Warm oil in a non-stick pan and add the makhana, toss for 2-3 minutes.
7. Add wasabi powder and toss again.
8. Transfer to a cutting glass, filled till top and serve.
9. Masala Papad Warm a non-stick tawa.
10. Cut the papad into half and roast on the heated tawa until cooked.
11. Fold into a cone, once cooked.
12. Add boiled chana, onion, tomato, green chilli, black salt, chaat masala, salt to taste, chopped coriander and lemon juice in a bowl and stir until combined.
13. Fill this mixture in the prepared papad cones.
14. Garnish with sev and coriander leaves, place the cones in a cutting glass and serve.
15. Cheesy Nuts Warm sufficient oil in a kadai and deep-fry the cashews and almonds till golden.
16. Drain on an absorbent paper and transfer to a bowl.
17. Add cheese powder, black salt, salt to taste and chaat masala and stir until combined.
18. Transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
19. Cheeseling Bhel Add cheeselings, tomatoes, onion, raw mango, black salt, salt, lemon juice and chaat masala in a bowl and stir until combined together.
20. Add chopped coriander and sev.
21. Transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
22. Schezwan Chakli Add rice flour, ground split skinless black gram (urad dal aata), schezwan sauce, salt to taste, cumin seeds, chilli powder and some water in a bowl.
23. Knead it to make soft dough.
24. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
25. Grease the chakli press with some oil.
26. Put some dough, at a time, in a chakli press and press out chaklis in strips directly in the oil.
27. Deep-fry on medium heat till crisp.
28. Drain on absorbent paper and cool down to room temperature.
29. Serve in a cutting glass.

Chana Cheese Roti Turnover Mix
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A satisfying fusion of Indian and Western cuisines, featuring a savory turnover made with roti crust filled with a hearty mixture of spiced chickpeas and cheese.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 8-10 hour Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1½ cups chickpeas (kabuli chane), soaked overnight, drained and boiled with salt and drained 2-
• 3 tablespoons oil + to drizzle
• 1 tablespoon grated ginger
• 1 large onion, finely chopped
• 4 whole wheat tortillas or rotis Chana jor garam as required Cheese cubes as required Ginger strips for garnish Fresh mint sprigs for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm oil in a non-stick pan, add ginger and fry gently till raw flavour goes away.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add onion and fry gently till it turns golden brown.
4. Add capsicum and fry gently for 1 minute.
5. Add chickpeas, red chilli powder, turmeric powder, coriander powder and salt.
6. Stir until combined.
7. Crush the chickpeas slightly with a masher.
8. Transfer the mixture into a bowl.
9. Chop coriander leaves and add to the chickpea mixture and stir until combined.
10. Allow the mixture to cool down.
11. Keep each roti on the worktop, place some mixture on one side, place chana jor garam and cheese cubes on top.
12. Fold the roti into half.
13. Warm a non-stick tawa, place the folded rotis on it, drizzle some oil all around and cook on medium heat till the underside becomes crisp.
14. Turnover and cook until the other side becomes evenly golden and the cheese melts.
15. Cut into half and transfer on a serving plate.
16. Sprinkle some red chilli powder, crush some chana jor garam and sprinkle on top.
17. Serve warm, garnished with ginger strips and mint sprigs.

Homestyle Cheese Kurkure
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Got guests coming over for Diwali? This recipe will come in handy. Mozzarella and cheddar cheese stuffed rolls deep-fried till the outside is crisp and the cheese inside has melted. These are a...

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 3 tablespoons grated mozzarella cheese
• 0 grams processed cheese, cut into ½ cm small cubes
• 1 cup +
• 2 tablespoons yogurt
• 1 tablespoon oil + for deep-frying
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take 1 cup flour in a bowl.
2. Add salt, baking soda, yogurt and some water and knead into a soft dough.
3. Add oil and knead well.
4. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
5. Set aside for 5-10 minutes.
6. Take mozzarella cheese in a bowl.
7. Add processed cheese, dried oregano, chilli flakes, crushed peppercorns and salt and lightly mix.
8. Warm sufficient oil in a pan.
9. Divide the dough into equal portions, coat with some flour and roll them out to make thin discs.
10. Cut each disc into rectangles.
11. Take 1 tablespoon flour in another bowl.
12. Add 2-3 tablespoons water to make a thick slurry.
13. Apply some slurry on 2 sides of each rectangle.
14. Put some cheese mixture in the center, and fold, applying some slurry on the edges to make thick rolls.
15. Deep-fry the prepared rolls in hot oil till golden and crisp.
16. Drain on absorbent paper.
17. Line a serving platter with individual shot glasses and put some tomato ketchup in each glass.
18. Place one fried roll in each glass and serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Simple Cheesy Roesti
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A crispy Swiss delight, marrying golden-brown potatoes with gooey melted cheese for a comforting and indulgent treat.

Cuisine: Other Cuisines Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 26-30 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• ½ cup grated mixed cheese (processed, cheddar and mozzarella)
• 3 large potatoes, peeled
• 1 teaspoon crushed black peppercorns ⅓ cup refined flour
• 2 teaspoons butter Fresh parsley sprig for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Grate potatoes on a plate and soak in a bowl of ice cold water for 15-20 minutes.
2. Drain the potatoes thoroughly, put them on a kitchen towel and dab excess water.
3. Spoon into another bowl, add mixed herbs, red chilli flakes, salt, crushed black peppercorns and refined flour and stir until combined.
4. Divide the potato mixture into two portions.
5. Warm a non-stick pan and spread one portion of the potato mixture on it.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
7. Sprinkle cheese evenly on top and spread the remaining potato mixture over the cheese.
8. Add butter and let it cook until crisp and golden in colour.
9. Turn and cook similarly on the other side as well.
10. Turn it out onto a serving dish, garnish it with parsley sprig and serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Chickoo Walnut Kulfi with Salted Caramel Sauce Treat
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Chickoo and walnuts add a wonderful flavour and texture to this ice cream and the salted caramel sauce is an added attraction

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 7-8 hour Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 6 Sapodilla (chickoo), quartered, seeded and pureed with skin 1/
• 4 cup Walnuts, chopped
• 2 litres Whole fat milk 2-
• 3 tablespoons Milk powder 1/
• 2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
• 1 cup Khoya, grated 3/
• 4 cup Sweet condensed milk Salted caramel sauce
• 1 pinch Salt 1/
• 4 cup Sugar 3/
• 4 cup Fresh cream
• 1 tablespoon Butter

Method
1. Warm milk in a deep non-stick pan.
2. Once it comes to a boil, reduce heat and simmer, stirring continuously, till it reduces to half its original volume.
3. Scrape the sides of pan and put it back into the pan.
4. Grind walnuts to a fine powder.
5. Take milk powder in a bowl, add 4 tbsps of hot milk and stir until combined.
6. Add this mixture to the simmering milk and mix.
7. Add green cardamom powder and khoya and stir until combined.
8. Continue to simmer till the mixture thickens.
9. Switch the heat off, transfer the mixture into a bowl and set aside to cool down to room temperature.
10. Add condensed milk and walnut powder to the milk mixture and stir until combined.
11. Add sapodilla puree and stir until combined.
12. Spoon into a tall jug.
13. Pour the mixture into metal kulfi moulds till they are ¾ full.
14. Cover them with their lids and keep the moulds in the freezer for 2 hours or till semi set.
15. Now insert sticks into kulfi mixture and keep them in the freezer again till completely set.
16. To prepare salted caramel sauce, heat a non-stick pan, add sugar and caramelize it on low heat.
17. Gradually add cream and whisk continuously so that no lumps are formed.
18. Add salt and stir until combined.
19. Remove from heat.
20. Add butter and whisk again.
21. Demould the kulfis, place them in individual serving bowls and serve with salted caramel sauce.

Kitchen Chilli Chana Aloo
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This tangy preparation will satiate even the most discerning palate. Indo - Chinese preparation made with boiled brown Bengal gram and potatoes.

Cuisine: Indo- Chinese Course: Main Prep: 7-8 hour Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 3 tablespoons red chilli sauce
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder
• 2 tbsps schezwan sauce + to garnish
• 1 cup brown Bengal gram (kala chana), soaked overnight and boiled
• 2 medium potatoes (aloo), boiled and peeled
• 1 tablespoon oil
• 1 teaspoon cumin seeds
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped ginger
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 tablespoon soy sauce
• 1 tablespoon vinegar
• 2 tbsps roughly chopped spring onion greens + to garnish Fried noodles to garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm oil in a non-stick pan, add cumin seeds, garlic and ginger, stir until combined and fry gently for 2 minutes.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add onions, mix and fry gently till translucent.
4. Cut potatoes into small cubes, add and cook for 1-2 minutes.
5. Add red chilli sauce, soy sauce, vinegar, red chilli powder, Schezwan sauce and brown Bengal gram, stir until combined and cook for 1-2 minutes.
6. Add spring onion greens and toss.
7. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with spring onion greens, Schezwan sauce and fried noodles and serve warm.

Chocolate and Cheese Sandwich Delight
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A decadent marriage of sweet and savory, melting creamy cheese with rich chocolate between two slices of buttery bread for a truly indulgent bite.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 0-5 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: Taste Sweet Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• Chocolate syrup for drizzling

Chocolate and Coconut Dim sums Mix
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

You will definitely relish these dimsums in their sweet avatar

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 4 tbsps + for drizzling Chocolate syrup 1/
• 2 cup Fresh coconut, scraped
• 0 grams Readymade dim sum dough for brushi tablespoons Ghee 1/
• 4 cup Mixed dried nuts, coarsely powdered 1/
• 2 cup Khoya, grated

Method
1. Place a banana leaf in a steamer, pour sufficient water in the lower compartment and heat.
2. Warm 1 tbsp ghee in a non-stick pan, add powdered nuts and saute for a minute.
3. Add coconut, mix and saute till light brown.
4. Remove from heat and transfer the mixture into a bowl.
5. Add khoya and 2 tbsps chocolate syrup and stir until combined.
6. Divide the dim sum dough into medium equal portions and roll out each into a disc.
7. Place a spoonful of coconut mixture in the centre of each disc, brush a little water on the edges, seal and shape into dim sums.
8. Brush some ghee on the banana leaf, place the dim sums on it, cover the steamer and steam for 5-6 minutes.
9. Warm 1 tbsp ghee in another non-stick pan, add steamed dim sums and pan-fry till lightly browned on all sides.
10. Add 2 tbsps chocolate syrup and toss.
11. Spoon into a serving platter, drizzle some Hershey"s Chocolate Syrup on top and serve at once.

Chocolate Loaf Bomb
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Chocolate shavings and cheese stuffed between bread roundels and crumb fried till crisp

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 grams Dark chocolate shavings
• 6 White bread slices
• 0 grams Processed cheese, grated as required Refined flour slurry
• 2 cups Dried breadcrumbs to deep fry Oil for dusting Icing sugar

Method
1. Lightly roll the bread slices with a rolling pin.
2. Then cut the bread slices in roundels with a round cutter.
3. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
4. On each bread roundel, place some chocolate shavings in the center, top it up with some cheese, further place some more chocolate shavings on top.
5. Apply water on the edges of the bread roundel and cover with another bread roundel.
6. Press the edges to seal.
7. Dip this bread bomb in refined flour slurry and then coat with breadcrumbs.
8. Slide these bombs gently into hot oil and deep fry until golden brown and crisp.

Kitchen Chocolate Diya
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This Diwali you can make edible diyas which the kids will simply love

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• Khoya/mawa (
• 5 grams), Sugar (
• 2 cups +
• 1 tablespoon Khoya/mawa (
• 5 grams), grated 1/
• 2 cup Sugar (
• 5 grams)
• 2 tablespoons Cocoa powder 1/
• 8 teaspoon Liquid chocolate food colour 7-
• 8 Almonds, slivered and soaked in saffron water

Method
1. Warm a non-stick pan, add khoya and saute till it melts.
2. Add sugar and stir until combined and cook, on medium heat, stirring continuously, till sugar dissolves and mixture starts leaving the sides of pan.
3. Transfer a small portion of cooked mixture into a small bowl and the remaining mixture into another bowl.
4. Add cocoa powder to the mixture in the second bowl and stir until combined.
5. Add liquid chocolate food colour and stir until combined.
6. Refrigerate both mixtures for 10-15 minutes or till they are set.
7. Divide cocoa-khoya mixture into medium equal portions and shape each into a ball.
8. Flatten each ball slightly, raise the edges and shape into a diya.
9. Take small portions of the plain khoya mixture, roll and shape into vaatis.
10. Place them on the diyas.
11. Stick soaked almond slivers on vaatis to form jyot (flame).
12. Transfer the prepared diyas on a serving plate and serve.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Quick Chocolate Gulab Jamun
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Chocolate ganache balls stuffed into gulab jamuns give them a flavourful make over

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 1-1.30 hour Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 1 1/
• 2 cups Hariyali khoya, grated
• 2 cups Cottage cheese (paneer), grated
• 2 cups Sugar 1/
• 2 teaspoon Cornflour
• 2 tablespoons Refined flour (maida) 1/
• 4 teaspoon Baking soda 1/
• 4 teaspoon Green cardamom powder to deep fry oIL
• 1 tablespoon Milk to garnish Pistachio slivers

Method
1. To make sugar syrup cook 2 cups sugar and 2 cups water in a deep kadai on low heat.
2. Meanwhile take mawa and cottage cheese in a bowl.
3. Sift together cornflour, refined flour, baking soda and green cardamom powder into this bowl and knead lightly to make smooth dough.
4. Shape 10 small portions of the dough into balls.
5. Cover the rest of the dough with a damp muslin cloth and also cover balls with damp muslin cloth.
6. Add milk to the sugar syrup and let it boil.
7. Discard the scum that may rise to the top.
8. Switch off heat just before the syrup reaches one string consistency.
9. Keep the syrup warm.
10. Warm sufficient oil in a pan.
11. Make a dent in the centre of each ball, keep a chocolate ganache ball in it, gather the edges, seal and shape into a ball again.
12. To remove cracks, moisten palms with water and roll the balls between them.
13. Set aside covered with muslin cloth.
14. Place a muslin cloth over a strainer kept over a bowl and strain the sugar syrup.
15. Slide the balls into hot oil and deep-fry, on low heat, stirring constantly till golden brown.
16. Drain the fried jamuns and soak them in the lukewarm sugar syrup for 1 hour.
17. Drain and transfer the gulab jamuns into serving bowls.
18. Pour a few spoons of sugar syrup over the jamuns.
19. Garnish with pistachio slivers and serve at once.

4. Add to the same bowl.
5. Add chocolate chips, isabgol and honey and stir until combined.

7. Press to even out the top and refrigerate till set.
8. Cut into bars and serve.

Homestyle Chocolate Overnight Muesli
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Soy milk is not only rich in proteins but it can be tolerated by those who are allergic to lactose

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 7-8 hour Cook: 0-5 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 tablespoons cocoa powder 1¾ cups muesli + for garnish
• 2 ripe bananas
• 2 cups soy milk 6-
• 7 seedless dates, chopped
• 1 apple 8-
• 0 orange segments
• 2 tablespoons chia seeds
• 2 tablespoons honey Chopped mixed nuts for garnish 10-
• 2 black berries for garnish 2-
• 3 strawberries for garnish 8-
• 0 blueberries for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Kitchen Chocolate Pakode
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A decadent twist on the classic Indian snack, pakode. Chocolate bars batter fried and served garnished with rabdi and chocolate sauce.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup refined flour (maida) ½ cup cornflour ½ teaspoon cinnamon powder + to sprinkle A pinch of baking soda
• 1 tablespoon powdered sugar + for garnish ¼ teaspoon vanilla essence
• 1 tablespoon oil + to deep fry
• 8 frozen chocolate bars Cocoa powder for garnish Rabdi for garnish Chocolate sauce to drizzle
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Take refined flour, cornflour, cinnamon powder, baking soda, powdered sugar and vanilla essence in a bowl.
2. Add sufficient water and stir until combined to make a thick batter.
3. Add oil, stir until combined and set aside to rest for 10 minutes.
4. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
5. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
6. Coat the chocolate bars with batter and deep fry in hot oil till golden and crisp.
7. Drain on an absorbent paper.
8. Arrange them on a serving plate.
9. Sift cocoa powder and powdered sugar over them.
10. Sprinkle cinnamon powder, place a few spoonsful of rabdi in the centre and drizzle chocolate sauce.
11. Serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Creamy Chocolate Peanut Chikki
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Indulge in the perfect fusion of chocolate, peanuts, and jaggery with this chikki- a satisfying treat that brings joy to both children and adults alike!

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 51-60 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 cup cocoa powder
• 1 cup roasted peanut powder
• 1 cup chopped jaggery
• 1 tbsp ghee + for greasing

Method
1. Line a baking tray with parchment paper and grease a rolling pin with ghee.
2. Warm 1 tablespoon ghee in a non-stick pan, add jaggery and stir until combined.
3. Let the jaggery melt completely.
4. To test whether the syrup is ready, put a drop of it in cold water.
5. If it forms a firm ball then the syrup is ready.
6. Add cocoa powder and stir until combined, add roasted peanuts and stir until combined.
7. Pour the mixture into the lined tray and spread it evenly with the greased rolling pin.
8. Cut into squares while it is still hot.
9. Allow to cool and then break the squares.
10. Serve or store in an air-tight container.

Creamy Chocolate Sandwich Biscuits
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

The children will simply enjoy these biscuits sandwiched with chocolate icing and so will the adults

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 51-60 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 4 tablespoons Cocoa powder 1/
• 3 cup Butter 3/
• 4 cup Powdered sugar
• 1 1/
• 2 cups Refined flour (maida)
• 4 teaspoons Baking powder a pinch Salt 1/
• 4 teaspoon Vanilla essence
• 3 tablespoons Milk to sprinkle Granulated sugar For the icing 1/
• 4 cup Butter
• 1 cup Icing sugar
• 3 tablespoons Cocoa powder

Method
1. To make the dough, take butter and powdered sugar in a bowl and beat till light and fluffy.
2. Sift refined flour, baking powder, cocoa powder and salt directly in the same bowl and stir until combined.
3. Add vanilla essence and milk and stir until combined to form a dough.
4. Place a butter paper on the worktop, transfer the dough on it, cover with another piece of butter paper and roll out thinly.
5. Cut into 1 inch × 3 inch rectangles and arrange them on a greased baking tray keeping a little distance between each.
6. With the back of satay stick, make small holes at equal distance on each rectangle.
7. Refrigerate for 10-15 minutes.
8. Heat the oven at 180° C.
9. Place the tray in the preheated oven, sprinkle granulated sugar over the rectangles and bake for 7 minutes.
10. Transfer the biscuits onto a wire rack and allow to cool down completely.
11. Meanwhile, for the icing, take butter and icing sugar in another bowl and beat till light and fluffy.
12. Add cocoa powder and mix till well combined.
13. Apply the prepared icing evenly on half the biscuits, cover each of them with one of the remaining biscuits.
14. Arrange the biscuits on a serving platter and serve.
15. Or store in an airtight container to serve whenever needed.

Coconut Moong Broth
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Our 'Coconut Moong Broth' is a flavourful and nutritious option that brings together the best of both worlds. This high protein recipe, made with protein-rich moong dal and creamy coconut, results in a...

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Soups Cook: 15-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild

Ingredients
• 3 cups thin coconut milk ¾ cup soaked whole green gram (sabut moong)
• 1 tablespoon ghee
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic ½ tablespoon finely chopped ginger
• 1 medium onion, chopped
• 1 medium carrot, peeled and cut into small cubes ¼ cup sweetcorn kernels
• 1 fresh red chilli, diagonally sliced
• 2 tablespoons chopped spring onions greens Fresh coriander sprigs for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm ghee in a nonstick deep pan, add garlic, ginger and onion and fry gently till translucent.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.

7. Add crushed black peppercorns and red chilli and stir until combined.

Simple Corn Cheese Balls
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

They are satisfying snacks made from a mixture of sweet corn kernels, cheese, herbs, and spices, shaped into small balls, coated with breadcrumbs, and then deep-fried until golden and crispy.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup corn kernels, boiled
• 1 cup grated processed cheese
• 1 teaspoon carom seeds (ajwain)
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped ginger
• 2 green chillies, finely chopped ½ teaspoon turmeric powder ¼ teaspoon baking soda
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped ½ cup gram flour (besan)
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped fresh coriander leaves Oil for deep-frying and greasing Yellow capsicum strips for garnishing Green capsicum strips for garnishing Fresh coriander sprigs for garnishing Green chutney for serving
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Crush corn kernels slightly and put into a bowl.
2. Add cheese, carom seeds, ginger, green chillies, turmeric powder and baking soda and stir until combined.
3. Add onion, gram flour and red chilli paste and stir until combined.
4. Add salt and coriander leaves and stir until combined.
5. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.

Simple Corn Halwa
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This combination of corn kernels and khoya will result in a flavourful halwa that will satiate the most discerning palate

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 6-10 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup Corn kernels, boiled
• 4 tablespoons Ghee 1/
• 4 cup + to garnish Mixed nuts, chopped
• 2 tablespoons Semolina (suji/rawa)
• 1 1/
• 2 cups Khoyta (mawa) a pinch Saffron strands
• 1 teaspoon Green cardamom powder 1/
• 2 cup Jaggery, grated
• 1 cup Milk

Method
1. Put corn kernels into a blender jar and blend into a fine paste.
2. Warm ghee in a non-stick pan, add nuts and fry gently till lightly browned and crisp.
3. Add semolina, mix and fry gently till fragrant.
4. Add corn paste and cook for 4-5 minutes, stirring continuously.
5. Add khoya and mix till it melts.
6. Add saffron strands and green cardamom powder and stir until combined.
7. Add jaggery and mix till it melts.
8. Add milk, mix and cook until the mixture thickens.
9. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with nuts and serve warm.

Spiced Corn Sprouts Bhel

There is no kurmura in this bhel. It is made with corn kernels and mixed sprouts making it super healthy

Cuisine: Indian Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup sweet corn kernels, boiled ½ cup mixed sprouts
• 1 large potato, boiled and peeled
• 1 small beetroot, boiled, peeled and cut into small cubes
• 1 medium onion, chopped
• 2 tablespoons date and tamarind chutney
• 2 tablespoons green chutney ½ small raw mango, chopped
• 2 tablespoons lemon juice
• 2 tablespoons chopped fresh coriander leaves
• 3 tablespoons sev 6-
• 8 crisp papdi
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Boil water in a deep non-stick pan, add mixed sprouts and cook until the sprouts are cooked.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.

4. Drain sprouts and set aside to cool.

7. Add raw mango, lemon juice, coriander leaves and sev and stir until combined.

Couscous and Mixed Vegetable Toss with Orange Vinaigrette
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Combine couscous, colorful veggies, and a sweet orange vinaigrette into a vibrant salad, topped with fresh microgreens and edible flowers for a refreshing and elegant dish.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Salads Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet & Sour Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup couscous
• 1 orange segments ½ medium red capsicum, finely chopped ½ medium green capsicum, finely chopped ½ medium tomato, finely chopped
• 2 cups orange juice 2-
• 3 sunflower microgreens 2-
• 2 tablespoons olive oil
• 1 tablespoon sugar
• 1 teaspoon lemon juice
• 1 tablespoon chopped coriander Edible flowers for garnishing
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Put water in a bowl, add microgreens and iceberg lettuce leaves.
2. Keep in the refrigerator for 15-20 minutes.
3. Boil some water in a non-stick pan.
4. Put couscous in a large bowl, add salt pepper powder and 1 tablespoon olive oil and stir until combined.
5. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
6. Add boiling water and stir until combined.
7. Cover with cling film.
8. Set aside for 15 minutes.
9. Warm orange juice in a 2nd non-stick pan, add sugar and cook until thickens.
10. Spoon into a bowl.
11. Drain water from the salad leaves and microgreens.
12. Put couscous in another large bowl, add green and red capsicum, tomato, coriander, orange segments reserving some for garnishing and stir until combined.
13. Add orange vinaigrette and stir until combined.
14. Place a ring mould on a serving plate, put the prepared couscous salad at the bottom of the ring, put microgreens and iceberg lettuce leaves over it.
15. Gently remove the ring mould.
16. Arrange reserved orange segments and edible flowers on the sides and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Fresh Creole Mushroom Rice
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Rice cooked with mushrooms and some other ingredients in the Creole style.

Cuisine: American,,,, Course: Rice Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup Button mushrooms
• 2 cups Rice, cooked
• 1 tablespoon Oil
• 1 medium Onion, finely chopped
• 1 teaspoon Roasted cumin powder
• 1 teaspoon Paprika powder ½ teaspoon Sugar
• 2 medium Tomatoes, blanched and chopped
• 1 teaspoon Lemon juice
• 4 tablespoons Saffron (kesar) milk
• 1 tablespoon Butter
• 3 tablespoons Processed cheese, grated
• 2 tablespoons Fresh parsley, chopped
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Preheat oven to 180º C.
2. To make Creole sauce, heat oil in a non-stick pan.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
4. Add onion and fry gently till it turns translucent Add capsicum, mix and fry gently.
5. Add mushrooms, mix and fry gently on lower heat for a minute.
6. Add salt, cumin powder, paprika powder and sugar and stir until combined.
7. Add tomatoes and stir until combined.
8. Add ½ cup water, mix and cook on low heat for 2 minutes.
9. Add 1 tablespoon chopped olives and lemon juice.
10. Stir until combined and remove from heat.
11. To make buttered rice, heat butter in another non-stick pan.
12. Add rice and toss gently.
13. Add saffron milk and salt.
14. Stir until combined and let it get heated through and switch off the heat.
15. Spread the buttered rice in a glass baking dish.
16. Top with a layer of Creole sauce, cheese, remaining olives and chopped parsley.
17. Put the dish in the preheated oven and bake for 5-8 minutes.
18. Serve warm.
