Taazi Haldi aur Gajar ki Subzi
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A unique dish made with fresh turmeric and carrot which is luscious and easy to make. Fresh turmeric and carrots cooked with Indian spices and served.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Main Prep: 11-15 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 1/
• 2 cups fresh turmeric (taazi haldi), chopped
• 1 medium carrot, chopped 2-
• 3 tablespoons ghee
• 1 teaspoon cumin seeds 1/
• 4 teaspoon asafoetida
• 2 tablespoons crushed ginger-garlic-green chillies
• 4 cup yogurt
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder 1/
• 2 tablespoon cumin powder
• 1 tablespoon coriander powder 5-
• 6 tablespoons fresh coriander leaves, chopped

Method
1. Warm ghee in a non-stick kadai, add cumin seeds and saute till they change colour.
2. Add asafoetida and crushed ginger-garlic-green chillies, mix and saute for 1-2 minutes.
3. Add onion, mix and saute till translucent.
4. Add fresh turmeric and carrot, stir until combined and saute for 2-3 minutes.
5. Add salt, mix, cover and cook on medium heat for 6-8 minutes.
6. Add yogurt, red chilli powder, cumin powder, coriander powder, a little salt and 3 tbsps coriander leaves and stir until combined.
7. Cover and cook for 2-3 minutes or till ghee surfaces.
8. Add remaining coriander leaves and stir until combined.
9. Transfer the sabzi into a serving bowl, garnish with coriander leaves and serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Tandoori Phalo ka Guldasta Special
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A vibrant bouquet of tandoor-roasted fruits, bursting with flavors and colors, promising a unique culinary experience.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 16-20 minutes Cook: 6-10 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 medium pear
• 1 medium pineapple, peeled and cut into
• 1 inch cubes
• 1 medium red apple
• 2 medium ripe bananas
• 3 tablespoons malt vinegar
• 2 tablespoons mustard oil
• 1 tablespoon tamarind paste
• 1 tablespoon red chilli powder
• 1 teaspoon coriander powder ½ teaspoon black pepper powder ¼ teaspoon green cardamom powder
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Cut pear into 1 inch cubes and put into a bowl.
2. Add pineapple cubes.
3. Cut apple into 1 inch cubes, peel and cut the bananas into thick slices and transfer into the same bowl.
4. Take malt vinegar, mustard oil, tamarind paste, red chilli powder, coriander powder, black pepper powder, green cardamom powder, brown sugar and honey in another bowl.
5. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
6. Add salt and stir until combined.
7. Pour ¾ of this marinade over fruits and stir until combined.
8. String the cut fruits onto skewers in the following sequence: pear, apple, pineapple, banana and apple.
9. Warm a non-stick grill pan, place the skewers and grill the fruits on medium heat, rotating the skewers and drizzling remaining marinade over the fruits till they turn golden brown on all sides and grill marks appear.
10. Remove onto a serving plate.
11. Sprinkle chaat masala and roasted white sesame seeds, and drizzle honey.
12. Serve warm with green chutney, lemon wedge, onion rings and micro greens.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Teen Dal ke Dahi Bhalle Favourite
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Make the ultra popular Delhi ka chaat even more interesting by combining the magic of three Dals into Dahi Bhalle. Try this flavourful recipe to satiate your next chatpate cravings!

Cuisine: Punjabi Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 40-50 minutes Cook Time 10-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• ½ cup split skinless green gram (dhuli moong dal), soaked for
• 0 minutes and drained ½ cup split black gram (dhuli urad dal), soaked for
• 0 minutes and drained
• 2 tablespoons split Bengal gram (chana dal), soaked for
• 2 teaspoons ginger-green chilli paste Oil for deep frying
• 4 teaspoons castor sugar ¼ teaspoon asafoetida (hing)
• 2 cups chilled yogurt ½ teaspoon red chilli powder ½ teaspoon roasted cumin powder + to sprinkle A pinch of yellow chilli powder Date and tamarind chutney to drizzle Chopped fresh coriander leaves to sprinkle Green chutney to drizzle

Method
1. Grind all the dals with little water and transfer in a bowl.
2. Beat well to incorporate air.
3. Add salt, ginger-green chilli paste and stir until combined.
4. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
5. Apply some water on palm, take a portion of batter and make a hole in the centre.
6. Deep fry till golden brown and crisp.
7. Take hot water in a bowl.
8. Add 1 tsp castor sugar, salt and asafoetida and stir until combined.
9. Drain the bhallas and dunk directly in the water mixture.
10. Set aside for 5 minutes.
11. Squeeze excess water and transfer in a serving plate.
12. Allow to cool.
13. Add salt, remaining castor sugar, red chilli powder, roasted cumin powder, yellow chilli powder to yogurt and stir until combined.
14. Drizzle yogurt and date and tamarind chutney on bhallas.
15. Sprinkle coriander leaves and roasted cumin powder on top.
16. Drizzle green chutney and serve.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Tofu Bhurji Wrap with Pickled Veggies Favourite
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Tofu as is well known is a rich source of proteins and vegetables are full of vitamins so when you combine them to make a tasty wrap it is bound to be high in nutrition

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 7-8 hour Cook: 31-40 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 1 cup split green gram with skin (chilkewali moong dal), soaked for 6-
• 8 hours and drained ¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
• 0 grams silken tofu
• 1 tablespoon oil
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 small tomato, finely chopped
• 1 teaspoon cumin seeds 1-
• 2 green chillies, finely chopped ¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder ½ teaspoon asafoetida
• 1 cup vinegar
• 1 tablespoon sea salt
• 1 tablespoon sugar
• 1 medium carrot, cut into sticks
• 1 medium beetroot, cut into sticks
• 1 medium cucumber, peeled and cut into sticks

Method
1. For pickled vegetables, heat a non-stick sauce pan, add vinegar, 1 cup water, salt and sugar and bring the mixture to a boil Grind drained green gram with turmeric powder, broken green chillies, salt and some water to a smooth and semi-thick batter.
2. Spoon into a bowl.
3. Add carrot, beetroot and cucumber to the vinegar mixture, remove the pan from heat and set aside.
4. To make the bhurji, heat oil in another non-stick pan, add onion, tomato, cumin seeds and green chillies and fry gently well.
5. Warm a little oil in a non-stick tawa, pour some green gram batter and swirl to make cheela.
6. Cook turning sides till evenly done on both sides.
7. Make more cheelas similarly.
8. Add tofu to the onion tomato mixture and scramble it well, add turmeric powder, red chilli powder and asafoetida and stir until combined Add coriander leaves and salt, stir until combined and remove it from heat.
9. Place each cheela on the work table, keep a spoonful of tofu bhurji in the center and fold the edges from all the sides and secure it with a toothpick.
10. Pack them in the tiffin box with pickled vegetables.

Fresh Tofu Chilli Garlic
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A delectable and spicy vegetable dish made with tofu and bird’s eye chillies and garlic. Tofu tossed with some garlic and vegetables.

Cuisine: Indo- Chinese Course: Main Prep: 11-15 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 0 grams silken tofu 2-
• 3 bird's eye chillies
• 1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic 3-
• 4 tablespoons olive oil 1/
• 2 cup diced mixed capsicums 1/
• 2 small yellow zucchini, cut into triangles 1/
• 2 small green zucchini, cut into triangles 4-
• 5 small spring onions
• 1 tablespoon red chilli sauce 1/
• 2 teaspoon soy sauce
• 1 teaspoon red chilli flakes
• 3 teaspoons cornflour slurry
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm olive oil in a non-stick pan, add mixed capsicums and fry gently for 2-3 minutes.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Drain them on a plate.
4. Add yellow and green zucchini to the oil remaining in the same pan and fry gently for 2-3 minutes.
5. Roughly chop spring onions and bird"s eye chillies.
6. Add spring onions and garlic to the same pan and fry gently well.
7. Add red chilli sauce and soy sauce and stir until combined.
8. Cut tofu into 1 inch cubes and add to the pan.
9. Add bird"s eye chillies and toss the mixture well.
10. Add red chilli flakes and cornflour slurry, stir until combined and add the sautéed capsicums.
11. Toss gently and take the pan off the heat.
12. Spoon into a serving dish and serve warm.

Tomato and Basil Paneer Style
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Paneer cubes cooked in basil flavoued tomato sauce. Serve this flavourful recipe with crispy garlic bread.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Main Course Vegetarian Prep: 11-15 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Taste: Mild Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 4 medium tomatoes, blanched and peeled 10-
• 2 basil leaves
• 0 grams paneer (cottage cheese), cut into cubes
• 2 tablespoons oil
• 2 tablespoons chopped garlic 1/
• 2 tablespoons red chilli flakes 1/
• 4 cup cream
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Chop tomatoes and put in a blender jar.
2. Blend into a smooth puree.
3. Warm oil in a non stick pan, add garlic and fry gently for ½ minute.
4. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
5. Add onions and fry gently till lightly browned.
6. Add freshly pureed tomatoes and tomato puree and stir until combined.
7. Add salt and crushed black peppercorns and stir until combined.
8. Add paneer and stir until combined.
9. Add red chilli flakes, basil leaves and cream.
10. Stir until combined and cook for 1 minute.
11. Serve warm.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Comfort Turai Baskets
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Deep fried hollowed ridge gourds stuffed with paneer and vegetable mixture, sprinkled with mozzarella cheese and baked

Cuisine: Indian Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 26-30 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Tangy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 1 teaspoon to sprinkle red chilli flakes
• 1 cup cornflour
• 2 tablespoons butter
• 1 tablespoon garlic, chopped
• 1 teaspoon ginger, chopped
• 2 teaspoon oil + to deep fry
• 1 small carrot, finely chopped
• 2 medium tomatoes, pureed ¼ cup corn kernels, boiled ¼ cup green peas, blanched
• 1 small green capsicums, cut into 1/
• 2 inch pieces
• 1 teaspoon to sprinkle dried mixed herbs
• 0 grams cottage cheese (paneer), cut into
• 1 inch rectangles
• 1 lemon
• 2 tablespoons grated mozzarella cheese + to sprinkle Red cherry tomatoes to serve Micro greens to serve Edible flowers to serve Red radish, cut into thin strips to serve
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Peel the ridge gourds completely.
2. Trim the ends.
3. Cut it into half horizontally and scoop out the centre portion to make hollowed cylinders.
4. Place these cylinders in a bowl, add salt, sprinkle chaat masala and red chilli flakes and stir until combined.
5. Take cornflour in another bowl.
6. Add enough water and make thick slurry.
7. Warm butter in a non-stick pan, add garlic, ginger and fry gently for a minute.
8. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
9. Add 2 tsps oil, carrot and fry gently for 1-2 minutes.
10. Add tomato puree and fry gently till oil separates.
11. Add corn kernels, green peas, capsicum, salt, 1 tsp red chilli flakes and 1 tsp mixed herbs and stir until combined.
12. Add cottage cheese and fry gently for 2-3 minutes.
13. Spoon into a bowl.
14. Halve the lemon, squeeze its juice and add to the cooked mixture.
15. Stir until combined and set aside to cool down.
16. Heat the oven to 180˚C.
17. Warm sufficient oil in a kadai.
18. Squeeze out extra moisture from ridge gourds.
19. To make ridge gourd baskets, dip them in cornflour slurry, slide into hot oil and deep-fry till golden brown and crisp.
20. Drain on absorbent paper.
21. Arrange these baskets on a baking tray.
22. Add 2 tbsps mozzarella cheese to the cottage cheese mixture and stuff it into the baskets.
23. Sprinkle mozzarella cheese on top.
24. Keep the tray in the preheated oven and bake for 3-4 minutes or till cheese melts.
25. Arrange the baked ridge gourd baskets on a serving platter and sprinkle mixed herbs and red chilli flakes on top.
26. Serve warm with red cherry tomatoes, micro greens, edible flowers and red radish.

Spiced Turai Shami Sliders
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Turai and chana dal kebabs served on multigrain bread roundels – they are as flavourful as they look

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 1.30-2 hour Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 grams ridge gourd (turai), peeled and cut ½ inch cubes
• 1 tablespoon +
• 1 teaspoon ghee
• 1 teaspoon chopped garlic
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder ½ teaspoon turmeric powder ½ teaspoon chopped green chilli ¾ cup split Bengal gram (chana dal), soaked, boiled with turmeric and salt ½ cup dried breadcrumbs
• 4 toasted multigrain bread roundels 2-
• 3 iceberg lettuce leaves Ketchup mayo as required
• 4 cheese slices, cut into small roundels Red cherry tomatoes, halved for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm 1 tablespoon ghee in a deep non-stick pan, add garlic, ginger and asafetida fry gently for a minute.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add ridge gourd and fry gently for a minute.
4. Add salt and stir until combined.
5. Add red chilli powder, turmeric powder and green chilli and stir until combined.
6. Add chana dal and stir until combined.
7. Cook for 8-10 minutes.
8. Switch off heat.
9. Add breadcrumbs and stir until combined.
10. Set aside to cool.
11. Grind the mixture to a smooth paste and transfer it into a bowl.
12. Divide the mixture into equal medium sized portions and shape each portion into kebabs.
13. Warm remaining ghee in a non-stick shallow pan, add kebabs and shallow fry on medium heat, turning sides, till golden brown on both the sides.
14. Drain on absorbent paper.
15. Place bread roundels on worktop.
16. Tear lettuce leaves and place on top of each.
17. Drizzle ketchup mayo mixture and place a kebab on each.
18. Cover with cheese slice roundels.
19. Insert toothpicks with halved red cherry tomatoes and serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Turmeric Frozen Cream - SK Khazana
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

It’s fun to make ice cream at home and when made this way you will enjoy it all the more

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Desserts Prep: 6-7 hour Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 1 teaspoon Turmeric powder
• 1 cup Milk
• 1 1/
• 2 cups Fresh cream 1/
• 2 cup Condensed milk
• 1 cup Milk powder
• 2 teaspoons Milk masala mix 1/
• 2 cup Castor sugar (caster sugar) 12-
• 5 Almonds blanched and peeled 10-
• 2 Pistachios blanched and peeled 12-
• 5 Cashewnuts blanched for garnish Icing sugar for garnish Dried cranberries for garnish Pistachio powder

Method
1. Put milk, fresh cream, condensed milk and milk powder in a deep non-stick pan and mix till you get a smooth and lump free mixture.
2. Place the pan on heat.
3. Add milk masala and stir until combined.
4. Allow the mixture to come to a boil, lower the heat and let the mixture reduce slightly.
5. Add turmeric powder and stir until combined.
6. Cook on low heat for 4-5 minutes, mixing constantly.
7. Add sugar and stir until combined.
8. Cook until the sugar dissolves completely.
9. In a blender jar, add almonds, pistachios, cashewnuts and a little milk mixture.
10. Switch off the heat.
11. Blend the nuts to a fine paste, add it to the milk mixture and stir until combined.
12. Strain the mixture into a bowl and refrigerate for 1 hour.
13. Add the ice cream mixture into the cold ice cream churner bucket.
14. Place it on the churner and start the machine.
15. Let it churn for 20 minutes.
16. Transfer the mixture into an air tight container and freeze it for 4-5 hours.
17. To serve, keep a waffle cup on a serving plate, dust icing sugar all around it.Scoop out the ice cream into the waffle cup and sprinkle some dried cranberries, pistachio powder and garnish with mint sprig.
18. Serve at once.

Everyday Valacha Birda
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

A traditional Maharashtrian dish, it is often cooked on special occasion. Field beans cooked with Indian spices and served.

Cuisine: Maharashtrian Course: Main Prep: 4-5 hour Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1 cup sprouted val, soaked and peeled
• 2 teaspoons cumin seeds
• 2 medium onions, peeled 3/
• 4 cup scraped fresh coconut 5-
• 6 garlic cloves 1/
• 2 teaspoon turmeric powder
• 2 tablespoons oil
• 1 teaspoon mustard seeds
• 1 teaspoon cumin seeds
• 1 teaspoon asafoetida
• 3 kokum petals
• 1 tablespoon chopped jaggery Fresh coriander sprig to garnish

Method
1. Put cumin seeds, 1 quartered onion, coconut and sufficient water in a blender jar and blend well.
2. Add garlic cloves and blend to a fine paste.
3. Finely chop remaining onion and put it in a large bowl.
4. Add val, turmeric powder, red chilli powder and salt and stir until combined.
5. Add the ground paste and stir until combined.
6. Warm oil in a deep non-stick pan, add mustard seeds, cumin seeds and asafoetida and let the seeds splutter and change colour.
7. Add the val mixture and stir until combined.
8. Add 2 cups water and stir until combined.
9. Cover and cook for 6-8 minutes.
10. Remove the lid and stir until combined.
11. Add kokum petals and jaggery and stir until combined.
12. Cook on medium heat for 12-15 minutes.
13. Take it off the heat and rest for a few minutes.
14. Spoon into a serving bowl, garnish with coriander sprig and serve warm.

Simple Veg Chowmein
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

This is the popular Indo-Chinese noodles stir fry dish made with mixed veggies and some sauces. Go ahead if you are looking for something savoury yet flavourful

Cuisine: Indo- Chinese Course: Noodles and Pastas Prep: 21-25 minutes Cook: 11-15 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 cups boiled noodles 5-
• 6 button mushrooms, quartered 3-
• 4 babycorn, diagonally sliced and blanched 1/
• 4 cup mixed capsicum (red, green and yellow) diamonds
• 1 medium onion, thickly sliced
• 1 tablespoon sesame oil (til oil) 4-
• 5 garlic cloves, sliced
• 1 inch ginger, finely chopped 2-
• 1 teaspoon soy sauce
• 1 tablespoon red chilli sauce
• 1 teaspoon vinegar
• 1 teaspoon toasted sesame seeds (til), to garnish Diagonally sliced spring onion greens, for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm sesame oil in a non-stick pan, add garlic, ginger and spring onion bulbs, mix and fry gently for 1 minute.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add mushrooms and babycorn, mix and fry gently for 1 minute.
4. Add salt and mix.
5. Add mixed capsicums diamonds and onion, mix and fry gently for 1 minute.
6. Add noodles, soy sauce, red chilli sauce and salt, mix and cook for 2 minutes.
7. Add vinegar and 1 teaspoon sesame seeds and stir until combined.
8. Move onto a serving platter, garnish with spring onion greens and sesame seeds and serve warm.

Fresh Veg Club Sandwich
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Delight in layers of freshness and flavor with our Fresh Veg Club Sandwich, packed with crisp veggies and zesty spreads, perfect for a satisfying bite on-the-go.

Cuisine: Fusion Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 26-30 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Mild Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 white bread slices
• 2 cups shredded cabbage
• 2 medium carrots, peeled and cut into strips
• 2 medium green capsicums, cut into strips
• 1 tablespoon mixed herbs
• 6 tablespoons oil
• 0 grams cottage cheese (paneer), cut into squares Butter as required Ice berg lettuce leaves as required Mustard sauce as required Tomato slices as required Tomato ketchup to serve Potato chips to serve
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Place the bread slices in a toaster and toast till done.
2. Remove and set aside.
3. In a bowl, add cabbage, carrots, capsicum, and mayonnaise.
4. Stir until combined and set aside.
5. In another bowl, add crushed black peppercorns, salt and 4 tablespoons oil, stir until combined.
6. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
7. Add the paneer pieces in it and apply the prepared mix on them.
8. Warm remaining oil in a non-stick grill, place the paneer pieces and grill till done on both side.
9. Keep aside.
10. Apply butter on one side of the bread slices, place a few lettuce leaves, add prepared vegetable mixture and sprinkle some crushed black peppercorns and salt.
11. Place another slice of bread and apply mustard sauce, arrange the paneer pieces, add tomato slices, sprinkle crushed black peppercorns, salt and top it with a few lettuce leaves.

Wholesome Veg Mughlai Parantha
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Experience the royal flavors of Mughlai cuisine in every bite of our Wholesome Veg Mughlai Parantha, filled with rich, aromatic spices and delectable vegetarian fillings.

Cuisine: Indian Course: Snacks and Starters Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 21-25 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 1½ cups refined flour + dusting Salt to taste
• 3 tablespoons oil
• 1 tablespoon chopped garlic
• 1 tablespoon chopped ginger ½ tablespoon chopped green chillies
• 1 medium onion, finely chopped
• 1 small capsicum, finely chopped
• 1 small carrot, peeled and grated
• 1 teaspoon red chilli powder ½ teaspoon turmeric powder
• 1 teaspoon coriander powder
• 1 teaspoon ghee + for applying
• 0 grams cottage cheese (paneer), grated Tomato ketchup to serve
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. In a bowl, add flour, salt and sufficient water, knead to soft dough.
2. Drizzle 1 tablespoon oil and knead again.
3. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
4. Cover with a damp muslin cloth and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
5. Meanwhile, heat remaining oil in a non-stick pan; add garlic, ginger, and stir until combined.
6. Add green chillies and onion, cook until translucent.
7. Add capsicum, carrots and stir until combined.
8. Cook for 1-2 minutes.
9. Add red chilli powder, turmeric powder, coriander powder and stir until combined.
10. Cook for 1 minute.
11. Add 1 teaspoon ghee and cottage cheese, stir until combined.
12. Add salt and stir until combined.
13. Divide the prepared dough into equal portions and shape them into balls.
14. Dust flour on the worktop, and roll out a portion of the dough into a disc of medium thickness.
15. Place a portion of the prepared stuffing and bring the sides to the centre and flip it.
16. Roll out into a square parantha.
17. Warm a non-stick pan; place the prepared parantha on it.
18. Cook until both sides are done.
19. Apply ghee on both sides and cut into pieces.
20. Serve warm along with tomato ketchup.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Simple Vegetable Makhani
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

An extremely popular dish, Simple Vegetable Makhani is made with the classic rich and creamy gravy. Served with any Indian bread, this dish is finger-licking good

Cuisine: Punjabi Course: Main Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 26-30 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Spicy Difficulty: Moderate

Ingredients
• 0 grams baby potatoes, boiled and peeled
• 0 grams cottage cheese (paneer)
• 0 fresh button mushrooms, halved 15-
• 0 small Broccoli florets, blanced
• 2 inches ginger, roughly chopped 10-
• 5 garlic cloves
• 5 green cardamoms A few maceblades
• 2 teaspoons red chilli powder (deghi mirch powder) 1/
• 2 teaspoon crushed red chillies A few fresh coriander stems
• 4 tablespoons oil 1/
• 2 teaspoon cumin seeds 1/
• 2 teaspoon garam masala powder
• 1 teaspoon dried fenugreek leaves (kasuri methi), roasted and powdered
• 2 tablespoons honey
• 1 tablespoon butter
• 2 tablespoons fresh cream Ginger, cut into thin strips for garnish
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm a deep non-stick pan, add tomatoes, salt, ginger, garlic, green cardamoms, mace blades, red chilli powder, crushed red chillies and 3 cups water and mix.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add fresh coriander stems, cover and cook.
4. Warm 2 tbsps oil in a non-stick wok, add baby potatoes and salt and let them cook until light golden.
5. Cut cottage cheese into medium cubes and put them in a bowl.
6. Add the baby potatoes.
7. Add button mushrooms to the same wok and toss till they soften.
8. Transfer them into same bowl.
9. The tomatoes are fully done.
10. Strain the liquid into a bowl.
11. Transfer the tomatoes into a mixer jar, cool and grind into a smooth puree.
12. Warm 2 tbsps oil in the same wok, add cumin seeds and let them change colour.
13. Add the strained liquid and cook until it is reduced.
14. Strained the tomato puree into the wok, pressing the residue in the strainer to extract all the flavours.
15. Stir until combined.
16. Add the vegetables and cottage cheese and mix.
17. Add garam masala powder and dried fenugreek leaf powder and stir until combined.
18. Cook until the gravy thickens.
19. Add honey and stir until combined.
20. Take the broccoli florets in a serving and heat in the microwave.
21. Add butter and fresh cream to the wok and stir until combined.
22. Pour this over the broccoli.
23. Garnish with a few more broccoli florets and ginger strips and serve warm.

Veggie Patty Stack
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Sink your teeth into a symphony of flavors with our Veggie Patty Stack, featuring a hearty patty made from wholesome vegetables, nestled in a soft bun, and topped with fresh, crunchy veggies.

• For one portion ⅓ cup blanched green peas 1-

• 2 medium potatoes, boiled and peeled

• 2 tablespoons mayonnaise

• 1 cheddar cheese slice

Method

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Walnut Phirni in Chocolate Cups Mix
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Chocolate and walnuts make a great combination, and when walnut phirni is served in chocolate cups it is divine

Cuisine: Indian Course: Mithais Prep: 31-40 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 cup Walnuts
• 2 cups Dark chocolate, melted
• 1 litres Full fat milk
• 3 tablespoons Rice, soaked for
• 0 minutes and drained 3/
• 4 cup Sugar 1/
• 4 teaspoon Green cardamom powder a few strands Saffron to garnish Chocolate cigars to garnish Fresh mint sprigs

Method
1. To prepare chocolate cups, fill silicon moulds till ¾ full with melted dark chocolate.
2. Swirl to coat all sides and drain extra chocolate.
3. Keep them in the refrigerator for 5-10 minutes or till set.
4. Put walnuts in a grinder jar and grind coarsely.
5. Boil milk in a deep non-stick pan.
6. Transfer walnuts into a bowl.
7. Put drained rice in the same grind jar and grind coarsely.
8. Add both rice and walnuts to the boiling milk and cook, stirring continuously till the rice gets cooked and the mixture thickens.
9. Scrape the sides occasionally and stir until combined.
10. Stir continuously so that no lumps are formed.
11. Add sugar, green cardamom powder and saffron strands, mix and cook for 2-3 minutes on low heat.
12. Remove from heat and let it cool down to room temperature.
13. Demould chocolate cups from the silicon moulds and place on a serving platter.
14. Fill up each chocolate cups with phirni.
15. Garnish with chocolate cigars and mint sprig.
16. Serve at once.

──────────────────────────────────────────────────
Zarda Boondi Dum Rice
──────────────────────────────────────────────────

Sweet biryani made with sweet boondi, basmati rice and plenty of nuts

Cuisine: Indian Course: Rice Prep: 26-30 minutes Cook: 16-20 minutes Serves: 4 Taste: Sweet Difficulty: Easy

Ingredients
• 2 grams saffron strands
• 1 cup Sweet boondi
• 3 cups Basmati rice, cooked
• 2 tablespoons Ghee
• 2 tablespoons Chironjee / charoli 2-
• 3 tablespoons Cashewnuts, chopped
• 2 tablespoons Raisins 1/
• 4 Dried coconut, sliced
• 1 1/
• 2 cup Milk 1/
• 4 cup Castor sugar 1/
• 4 teaspoon Cinnamon powder 15-
• 0 Walnuts 1/
• 2 cup Khoya
• 1 + to garnish Fresh mint sprig 1/
• 2 Lemon to garnish Orange to garnish Rabdi
• A pinch of hing (asafoetida)

Method
1. Warm ghee in a deep stainless steel handi, add chironji, cashewnuts, raisins and dried coconut and fry gently on medium heat for 1-2 minutes.
2. Sprinkle a pinch of hing (asafoetida) and let it bloom for a few seconds.
3. Add milk, boondi, castor sugar, saffron and ½ tsp cinnamon powder, stir until combined and cook for 2-3 minutes, stirring occasionally.
4. After the mixture comes to a boil, add walnuts and stir until combined.
5. Cook for 1-2 minutes, add ¼ cup khoya, cooked basmati rice, mint leaves and remaining khoya.
6. Squeeze lemon juice into the handi and add remaining cinnamon powder, mix, cover and cook for 4-5 minutes on low heat.
7. Cut orange into round slices and halve them.
8. Transfer the biryani onto a serving platter, garnish with rabdi, orange slices and a mint sprig and serve.
